});

<script>
(function() {
    var KEY    = "vdx_modal_survey_2026";
    var DELAY  = 5 * 1000;
    var EXPIRE = 7;

    function isDismissed() {
        try {
            var item = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
            if (!item) return false;
            var data = JSON.parse(item);
            if (Date.now() > data.expires) { localStorage.removeItem(KEY); return false; }
            return true;
        } catch(e) { return false; }
    }

    function dismiss() {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify({ expires: Date.now() + EXPIRE * 864e5 }));
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    if (isDismissed()) return;

    var overlay = document.getElementById('vdxModalOverlay');
    var close   = document.getElementById('vdxModalClose');

    setTimeout(function() {
        overlay.classList.add('vdx-modal--visible');
    }, DELAY);

    close.addEventListener('click', function() {
        overlay.classList.remove('vdx-modal--visible');
        dismiss();
    });

    overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (e.target === overlay) {
            overlay.classList.remove('vdx-modal--visible');
            dismiss();
        }
    });
})();
</script>	   <!--  FOOTER LINKS -->
